When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [110] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拉普拉斯近似的后验不确定性估计方法，用于预训练分子扩散模型，通过测量生成轨迹中噪声预测的变异性来评估生成样本的质量，并利用该分数进行样本过滤以提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中被广泛使用，但缺乏判断生成分子质量高低的原则性信号，因此需要一种能够估计每个样本不确定性的方法，以识别低质量生成物。

Method: 提出一种后验方法，基于去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，测量整个生成轨迹中噪声预测的变异性，由此计算每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本级质量指标呈负相关，能够有效指示样本质量；通过该分数过滤生成样本可在测试时提升模型性能（测试时缩放）。

Conclusion: 该方法为预训练分子扩散模型提供了一种无需修改模型结构即可估计样本质量的后验不确定性估计手段，并可作为一种测试时缩放策略来提升生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [111] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将神经模型编辑形式化为强化学习问题的探索性框架，通过奖励反馈学习编辑策略，在掩码世界和偏移世界两种环境中分别进行乘性和加性权重修改，在文本分类的偏差缓解和图像分类的机器遗忘任务上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练神经网络的编辑需要专门定制的算法，设计这类算法耗时且需要大量精力，缺乏一个统一的、可学习的编辑范式。

Method: 将神经模型编辑建模为强化学习问题，智能体通过奖励反馈修改模型。设计了两个环境：MaskWorld（乘性缩放权重）和ShiftWorld（加性更新权重）。奖励函数结合了效用保持目标和任务特定编辑目标。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务上，遗忘集准确率降至近0%，保留集准确率保持90%以上；在偏差缓解任务上，偏差相关性能提升超过5%，同时保持通用分类效用。

Conclusion: 神经模型编辑可以转化为强化学习问题，编辑策略可以通过奖励反馈学习得到，无需为每个任务手动设计专门算法。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [112] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof 是一个面向竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，通过将证明生成、验证和修复能力整合到单一 M3 模型中，结合锦标赛选择机制搜索候选证明群体，使模型在 IMO 2025 和 USAMO 2026 上分别达到 35/42 和 36/42，超越了人类金牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决竞赛级数学证明中，单一模型生成的证明可靠性不足、难以通过测试时扩展提升性能的问题，实现超越人类金牌水平的高质量自动证明。

Method: 首先训练三种面向证明的能力——证明生成、证明验证和基于批评的证明修复，采用低假阳性率的深度防御生成式验证器。将这些能力合并为单一 M3 模型。测试时，MaxProof 将模型视为生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，在候选证明群体中进行搜索，通过锦标赛选择返回最终证明。

Result: M3 模型在 IMO 2025 上达到 35/42，在 USAMO 2026 上达到 36/42，均超过人类金牌阈值。

Conclusion: MaxProof 的测试时扩展策略显著提升了竞赛级数学证明的性能，证明了群体级搜索和多重能力整合的有效性。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [113] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个超分子化学基准测试SupraBench，包含四个核心推理任务和一个辅助视觉任务，以及一个1600万token的领域语料库SupraPMC。实验表明，现有LLMs在所有任务上表现欠佳，领域自适应预训练在分布内回归任务上有效但会牺牲严格的格式输出能力，不同任务族的难度分布差异揭示了当前超分子化学推理的具体缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分子化学中的主客体系统设计耗时且验证成本高，尽管LLMs在分子结合任务上表现出色，但目前缺乏系统性评估LLMs在超分子化学推理能力（如结合亲和力预测）的基准测试。

Method: 与领域专家合作构建SupraBench，设计四项核心任务（结合亲和力预测、最优结合剂选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）及一项基于视觉的辅助任务；同时从Europe PMC蒸馏构建16M token的SupraPMC语料库，用于领域自适应预训练。对多种开源和闭源LLMs进行基准测试。

Result: 现有LLMs在所有任务上均存在较大提升空间；领域自适应预训练在分布内回归任务上迁移效果良好，但会与严格的字母格式输出产生权衡；不同任务族的难度分布差异显著，揭示了当前超分子化学推理的不同失败模式。

Conclusion: LLMs在超分子化学推理任务上仍有显著不足，需要进一步改进；SupraBench和SupraPMC为评估和提升LLMs在该领域的能力提供了系统化平台。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [114] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一个全无监督的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，通过K=500个跨四种小波族的随机解析特征抽取，结合五个结构化孤立森林（每个异常类型一个加一个元检测器），利用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值校准，在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上实现平均F1=0.228，VUS-PR达0.463（超此前最佳40.7%），并能直接提供异常类型归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列中存在四种结构不同的异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时间异常和集体异常），每种需要不同的特征表示，但现有无监督方法大多只针对一两种类型且可解释性有限。

Method: 生成K=500个跨四个小波族（Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet）的随机解析小波特征，每种针对特定异常类型；构建五个结构化孤立森林（每类型一个加一个元IF处理复合异常）；使用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值校准检测，无需数据集特定调参。

Result: 在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上，平均F1=0.228（全部数据集）和F1=0.322（13个可检测数据集）；VUS-PR达0.463，超此前最佳0.329（+40.7%），在25种方法中排名第一。消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38% F1）、四分支结构（+20%）和元IF（+23%）均至关重要。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF是首个同时覆盖四种异常类型且无需数据集特调的无监督框架，具备直接异常类型归因能力。诊断框架识别出6/19数据集对任何无监督方法均不可检测。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [115] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现了任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，并理论上保证了收敛到奖励倾斜的序列分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型已扩展到任意长度序列生成，但原则性的奖励引导微调方法尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，建立了去掩码和插入质量作为最小化解码误差的可行方法，并提出自适应联合解码(AJD)损失函数，可证明地生成奖励倾斜分布的最优路径测度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性方面优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调提供了统一的理论框架，在不需要目标样本的情况下实现了可证明的收敛保证。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [116] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整杯积神经层，通过硬编码杯积和高阶规范理论的调整项，实现了规范不变的读出。理论表明在闭链上输出仅依赖于调整系数，且该系数为零时输出完全消失，证明调整是规范不变信号的唯一来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理和几何中许多重要可观测量是上链的杯积，但现有神经层缺乏规范不变性保障，需要设计一种天然具有规范不变性的神经原语。

Method: 提出调整杯积神经层，将杯积与高阶规范理论的调整项硬编码为神经原语，通过设计使读出天然满足规范不变性。

Result: 理论证明在闭链上输出完全取决于调整系数；当调整系数为零时，无论其他参数如何输出完全消失；该可观测量是非零二次型，且在1次和2次规范变换下精确不变。

Conclusion: 调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，该设计为物理和几何中规范不变可观测量的神经建模提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [117] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重新定义为未来可观测性推断和值估计的联合问题，通过观测流和值流双流结构及三个专用模块显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互，在包含自然缺失和工业数据的Shadow基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法在处理不完整和不规则数据时，隐式假设推理时未来有效观测的时间戳已知，这限制了实际应用价值。现实中更关键的问题不仅是未来值是多少，还包括未来是否会有有效观测发生。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架：(1) 双流结构：观测流（Observation Stream）和值流（Value Stream）；(2) 三个耦合模块：可靠性感知嵌入（Reliability-aware Embedding）、观测引导的依赖建模（Observation-guided Dependency Modeling）、联合预测（Joint Prediction）；(3) 构建Shadow基准数据集；(4) 提出观测-值联合熵（OVJE）评估指标。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies在各类指标上一致优于现有方法，证明了显式建模未来可观测性对于含缺失值的时间序列预测的重要性。

Conclusion: 未来时间序列预测应同时考虑值估计和观测可推断性，Timeflies通过双流架构有效解决了这一问题，为不规则时间序列预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [118] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SCSB（单纯形约束稀疏装袋）的数学严谨框架，用于对基于自举法的装袋集成进行后训练压缩与概率校准。通过引入凹二次惩罚项解决L1-单纯形悖论，在概率单纯形上联合优化集成剪枝与校准，实现高达96%的集成压缩、线性推理加速和更优的概率校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准装袋集成（如随机森林、装袋SVM和装袋神经网络）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，这种朴素均匀先验忽略了基估计器在不同局部区域的差异性能力，导致模型过度自信。现有方法缺乏同时实现集成压缩与概率校准的数学严格框架。

Method: 将集成剪枝与校准形式化为在概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，通过最小化袋外（OOB）损失来求解。针对L1范数在单纯形上为常数、无法有效剪枝的"L1-单纯形悖论"，引入凹二次惩罚项以诱导稀疏性。

Result: SCSB是模型无关的，实现了高达96%的集成压缩，获得线性推理加速，同时降低期望校准误差（ECE），提升概率校准质量，并保持或增强泛化准确率。

Conclusion: SCSB框架成功解决了装袋集成中均匀投票权重的缺陷，通过数学严格的优化方法同时实现了高效的模型压缩和可靠的概率校准，且具有良好的模型通用性。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过早期退出方法估计Chain-of-Thought推理中每一步的因果重要性，发现推理过程中存在一个"承诺边界"——从临时性中间猜测到稳定高置信度答案的急剧转变，这一转变通常在单个步骤中完成且早于推理块结束，后续步骤属于"附带现象"（不影响最终答案概率）。利用注意力探针可线性解码答案形成阶段，并据此在承诺边界处提前退出推理块，平均减少最多55%的CoT长度而对模型性能影响微乎其微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对Chain-of-Thought推理中每个步骤对最终答案的因果影响理解不足，缺乏量化各步骤重要性的方法，也无法确定答案何时在推理过程中真正形成。

Method: 使用早期退出（early exit）方法估计推理每一步的因果重要性，识别"承诺边界"；采用注意力探针（attention probes）从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段，并基于承诺边界实现推理块的早期退出。

Result: 推理通常存在一个承诺边界，该转变在单步内完成且早于推理块结束；后续CoT步骤是附带现象（不改变答案概率）；注意力探针可高精度线性解码答案形成阶段，并泛化到未见过的推理任务；基于此信号早期退出可平均减少最多55%的CoT长度而性能几乎无损。

Conclusion: Chain-of-Thought推理中存在可检测的承诺边界，后续步骤多为冗余的附带现象，通过早期退出可大幅提升推理效率而不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [120] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续学习中的灾难性遗忘现象，提出稳定恢复流形假说，发现遗忘知识在表示重组后仍可紧凑解码，灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题而非信息破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可访问性崩溃框架，探究持续学习中可恢复性的几何结构，验证遗忘究竟是信息破坏还是可访问性问题。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上使用顺序训练的ResNet-18，提出恢复子空间维度(k_t)度量，分析10个任务的可恢复性、表示漂移和恢复复杂度。

Result: 恢复维度保持稳定(均值k_t=8.0)，主角度漂移强预测可恢复性(r=-0.862)，几何模型解释82.2%的可恢复性方差。

Conclusion: 支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非先前知识的破坏。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [121] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的更新特性，发现其更新具有稀疏性和频谱集中性，且主要由FFN层承载；更新落在源权重接近零的坐标上，保留了在线策略后训练的关键几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OPD作为后训练方法效果显著，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不明确，作者希望通过分析OPD更新的稀疏性和几何特性来理解其工作原理。

Method: 在多个语言和视觉-语言模型对上，通过分析OPD更新的稀疏性（层分布、FFN集中性）、数值秩、频谱集中性以及与源权重的几何关系，并进行SGD与AdamW优化器的消融实验。

Result: （1）OPD更新具有稀疏性，分布在各层且集中在FFN上；仅训练发现的子网络可恢复OPD性能。（2）SGD在此场景下不如AdamW，因为密集教师监督保留了异构梯度尺度。（3）OPD更新数值上满秩但频谱集中，主要偏离源权重的主奇异子空间，且落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写，OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [122] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络中两种主流位置编码（谱编码和游走编码）在截断情况下的表达能力差异。理论证明截断后不同编码家族的表达能力根本不同，截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试；实验表明混合使用多种截断编码优于单一编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中广泛使用截断版本的位置编码（如前k个特征向量或邻接矩阵的k次幂），但这些截断编码的理论性质尚不明确，亟需研究其在截断条件下的表达能力。现有理论认为谱编码和游走编码表达能力等价（介于1-WL和3-WL之间），但该结论建立在完整版本（O(n³)复杂度）基础上，与实用场景脱节。

Method: 理论分析：研究截断后不同位置编码家族的区分能力差异，特别分析截断谱编码与1-WL测试的关系；引入k-调和距离这一谱编码家族进行对比。实验验证：在真实数据集上比较单一截断编码与混合编码的性能。

Result: 理论发现：截断后谱编码和游走编码的表达能力不再等价，存在根本性差异；截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试。实验发现：混合使用多种截断编码在真实数据集上优于任何单一家族。

Conclusion: 截断位置编码的理论性质与完整版本有本质区别，实践中最优策略是混合使用多种截断编码而非依赖单一家族。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [A Communication Complexity Lower Bound for Nonuniformly Convex Consensus Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12675)
*Demyan Yarmoshik,Maxim Klimenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了时变网络下凸分散优化的通信复杂度，证明了非均匀正则性条件下无法达到均匀正则性条件下的轮复杂度下界Ω(χ_𝒢 √κ_g log(n/χ_𝒢) log(1/ε))，其中χ_𝒢是网络拉普拉斯条件数，κ_g是全局目标条件数。构造基于谱图理论，通过将时间旋转星形装置嵌入扩展图边并修补以保持谱连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时变网络下凸分散优化问题的通信轮数下界，特别是非均匀正则性条件下是否能够达到均匀正则性条件下的最优复杂度。

Method: 利用谱图理论，将时间旋转星形(time-rotating star)装置嵌入到扩展图(expander)的边中，并修补它们以保持谱连通性，构建下界证明。

Result: 证明了非均匀正则性条件下通信轮数的下界为Ω(χ_𝒢 √κ_g log(n/χ_𝒢) log(1/ε))，表明均匀正则性条件下的可达复杂度在非均匀条件下不可匹配。

Conclusion: 非均匀正则性条件下的通信复杂度严格高于均匀正则性条件，时变网络的谱属性（拉普拉斯条件数）对分散优化复杂度有根本性影响。

Abstract: We study the communication complexity of convex decentralized optimization over time-varying networks, where $n$ nodes hold private functions and must agree on the global minimizer using only synchronous exchanges with neighbors. The cost is the number of communication rounds to reach accuracy $\varepsilon$ -- a measure akin to round complexity in the LOCAL model, but constrained by nodes sharing only oracle responses. We prove a new lower bound of $Ω\!\left(χ_{\mathcal G} \sqrt{κ_g}\,\log\frac{n}{χ_{\mathcal G}}\log\frac1\varepsilon\right)$ communication rounds, where $χ_{\mathcal G}$ is the condition number of the network Laplacians and $κ_g$ that of the global objective, showing the round complexity attainable under uniform regularity cannot be matched in the nonuniform regime. The construction rests on spectral graph theory: we embed time-rotating star gadgets into the edges of an expander and patch them to preserve spectral connectivity.

</details>


### [124] [Abstract Dynamic Programming on Partially Ordered Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12777)
*Chengyuan Peng,John Stachurski,Jingni Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究偏序空间上的抽象动态规划，将序论方法与拓扑和度量基础相结合，证明策略算子的拓扑稳定性（全局稳定性和压缩性）可带来动态规划的基本最优性性质以及值函数迭代、Howard策略迭代和乐观策略迭代的收敛性，并在极弱假设下证明平稳策略优于非平稳策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将序论方法（偏序空间上的抽象动态规划）与拓扑和度量基础相结合，为动态规划提供统一的理论框架，并为结构估计、最优停止、贝叶斯序贯分析等应用问题提供收敛性保证和更弱的假设条件。

Method: 在偏序空间上定义抽象动态规划，引入策略算子的拓扑稳定性概念（全局稳定性和压缩性），基于这些性质推导值函数迭代、Howard策略迭代和乐观策略迭代的收敛性，并证明平稳策略对非平稳策略的支配性。

Result: 证明了拓扑稳定性（全局稳定性和压缩性）足以保证动态规划的基本最优性性质及三类迭代算法的收敛性；在极弱假设下证明了平稳策略优于非平稳策略；在最优停止和贝叶斯序贯分析中弱化了现有假设并扩展了算法保证。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了偏序与拓扑方法，为动态规划理论提供了更一般的理论基础，并在多个应用场景中改进了现有结果。

Abstract: We study abstract dynamic programs on partially ordered spaces, pairing the order-theoretic approach to dynamic programming with topological and metric foundations. We show that readily verifiable forms of topological stability, such as global stability and contractivity of the policy operators, deliver the fundamental optimality properties of dynamic programming together with convergence of value function iteration, Howard policy iteration, and optimistic policy iteration. We also prove that stationary policies dominate nonstationary policy plans under very weak assumptions. Applications include Markov decision processes, structural estimation problems in which maximization and integration are interchanged, optimal stopping without discounting, and Bayesian sequential analysis. For the last two, our results weaken existing assumptions and extend algorithmic guarantees for foundational problems.

</details>


### [125] [Maximum Utility Split Method for Utility Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12868)
*Bo Chen,Jia Liu,Huifu Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最大效用分割（MUS）的方案，通过迭代设计问卷逐步缩小效用函数的模糊集，最终收敛到真实效用函数，并提出了基于区间算法和线性规划的实现方法，数值实验验证了其在高维效用函数和S型效用函数上的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的随机效用分割（RUS）方案在效用函数模糊缩减效率上存在局限，需要一种更高效的方法来加速效用函数的识别过程，以满足实际决策支持系统（如智能投顾）的需求。

Method: 提出MUS方案：设计一个两固定结果但概率可变的彩票与一个确定性结果彩票，通过最大化结果间效用值范围来确定确定性结果，并设置随机概率使效用模糊集范围在该点减半。采用基于区间算法的线性规划序列求解来定位最大效用范围点，并通过Kantorovich度量下的极小/极大效用函数确定名义效用函数。

Result: 在适度条件下，MUS能够生成一系列问卷，有效减少模糊集并收敛到真实效用函数。数值结果表明MUS方法在效用函数识别效率上优于传统方法，基于MUS查询的智能投顾系统性能良好。该方案成功扩展到S形等非凹效用函数。

Conclusion: MUS方案通过最大化效用范围的分割策略，显著提高了效用函数模糊集的缩减效率，为决策分析和智能投顾等领域提供了一种高效的效用函数识别方法，并具有良好的扩展性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a new approach, called maximum utility split (MUS) scheme, which is built on random utility split (RUS) scheme but with a notable difference: one lottery is designed with two fixed outcomes but with varying probability, and the other has a deterministic outcome specifically chosen at the point where the range between the largest and smallest possible utility values is maximized. Consequently, the probability of random lottery is set such that the range of the ambiguity set of utility functions is reduced by half at the point. Under moderate conditions, we show that MUS can successively generate a sequence of such questionnaires and effectively reduce the ambiguity set, eventually converging to the true utility function as the number of questionnaires increases. The main challenge is to effectively identify the point with the largest utility range for a given ambiguity set constructed from preference information. Based on the structure of the ambiguity set, we propose an interval-based algorithm which identifies each certain-outcome lottery by solving a sequence of linear programs. Moreover, to deal with the case where elicitation terminates before the ambiguity set reduces to a singleton, we demonstrate how to figure out a nominal utility function by solving optimization programs. These identify the smallest and largest utility functions under the Kantorovich metric within the ambiguity set, after which we identify a nominal utility function located in the middle of them. Finally, numerical results demonstrate the efficiency of the MUS method and the performance of a robo-advisor system based on MUS-type queries and the nominal utility elicitation. While the main discussions focus on concave utility functions, we also demonstrate how the MUS approach can be extended to accommodate general non-concave utility functions, particularly S-shaped ones.

</details>


### [126] [Randomized Optimal Switching Problem and Related Mirror Descent Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12875)
*Yuchao Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间强化学习在最优切换问题中的应用，通过KL散度正则化和温度参数λ将确定性切换松弛为随机化框架，证明了正则化值函数是椭圆HJB系统的唯一光滑解，给出了显式最优Gibbs策略，并建立了与经典最优值函数的误差界（O(λ log(1/λ))）。同时提出了镜像下降流算法，给出了常温和退火调度下的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决连续时间最优切换问题中经典确定性切换控制的探索性不足，通过随机化框架和熵正则化使问题可解且可学习。

Method: 将确定性切换控制松弛为连续时间马尔可夫链驱动的随机化框架，引入KL散度正则化（温度参数λ），推导出基于值函数差分的指数变换给出的最优Gibbs策略；设计镜像下降流算法在双对数策略空间中求解。

Result: 正则化值函数是椭圆HJB系统的唯一光滑解；与经典最优值函数的误差为O(λ log(1/λ))；镜像下降流下，常温度调度收敛率O(1/(e^{λs}-1)+λ log(1/λ))，退火调度λ_s=1/√(1+s)下收敛率O(log s/√s)。

Conclusion: 所提出的熵正则化框架和镜像下降流算法为连续时间最优切换问题提供了一种理论完备且数值可行的强化学习解法，误差界与现有熵正则化控制问题一致且被认为是紧的。

Abstract: We study continuous-time reinforcement learning for the optimal switching problem, in which a decision-maker controls a diffusion process by switching among finitely many regimes, incurring both running and transition costs. To enable exploration, we relax the classical deterministic switching control to a randomized framework, where the switching decisions are governed by a continuous-time Markov chain with state-dependent generator, and augment the cost functional with a KL-divergence regularization weighted by a temperature parameter $λ$. Under mild assumptions on the coefficients, we establish that the regularized value function is the unique smooth solution of an elliptic Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman system, and derive an explicit optimal Gibbs policy given by an exponential transformation of the value function differences across modes. We further prove that the regularized value function approximates the classical optimal value function with error of order $O\left(λ\log \frac{1}λ\right)$, which is consistent with analogous bounds established in other entropy-regularized control problems and is believed to be sharp. To solve the regularized problem numerically, we introduce a mirror descent flow in the dual logarithmic policy space, prove its well-posedness and the monotonic decrease of the value function along the flow, and establish quantitative error bound to the classical optimal value function. For a constant temperature scheduler, the convergence rate is of order $O\left(\frac{1}{e^{λs} - 1}+λ\log\frac1λ\right)$, while under the annealing scheduler $λ_s = \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+s}}$, we obtain the rate $O\left(\frac{\log s}{\sqrt{s}}\right)$, which decays to zero as the flow time $s \to \infty$.

</details>


### [127] [Flyby Distance Pursuit for Guarding a Target with an Inferior Guard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13012)
*Navot Israeli,Vitaly Shaferman,Oded Golan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的微分博弈模型，通过引入记录最小距离的辅助状态变量和终端约束条件，解决了较慢防御者拦截较快攻击者的目标防护问题，并数值求解了多种解类型，揭示终端速度与飞越距离之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有追击博弈存在结构局限性，无法有效处理攻击者必须以目标为终点且防御者速度较慢的拦截问题，需要一种新的博弈公式来嵌入攻击任务约束并评估捕获可能性。

Method: 将问题建模为微分博弈，引入记录双方最小分离距离的辅助状态变量；采用间接法构建两点边值问题，通过数值方法求解多种解类型。

Result: 当攻击者仅需到达目标时，可逃脱捕获但导致长飞行时间和低终端速度；施加终端速度约束后得到更简化解，并揭示了终端速度与飞越距离的权衡关系，在相关参数空间内捕获是可能的。

Conclusion: 通过新颖的终端条件与辅助状态变量组合，突破了现有追击博弈的结构限制，开辟了一类新的可解问题，并为评估攻击任务约束下的捕获可行性提供了方法。

Abstract: Guarding a target against a fast Attacker with a slower Guard is posed as a differential game. Both players follow nonlinear, thrust-free, aerodynamic motion in the plane. The capturability problem inherent in this scenario is addressed by formulating the game such that the Attacker is constrained to terminate at its Target, and the game value is the flyby distance. The latter is made accessible at termination by introducing an auxiliary state variable that records the players' minimum separation distance throughout their flight. This novel combination of termination condition and auxiliary state removes a structural limitation of current pursuit games and opens a new class of solvable problems. It also enables us to embed attacker mission constraints directly. Taking an indirect approach, we formulate the game's Two-Point Boundary Value Problem, solve it numerically, and identify several solution types. When the Attacker's only constraint is to reach the Target, he can do so without being captured. These types of solutions, however, are less practical due to long flight times and slow terminal speeds. Imposing an Attacker terminal speed constraint yields simpler solutions and reveals the trade-off between terminal speed and flyby distance. We demonstrate that in such a setting, capture is possible in a relevant region of the parameter space.

</details>


### [128] [A Survey of Community Detection from an Operations Research Perspective: Taxonomy, Mathematical Formulations, Modularity Functions, and Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13031)
*Miguel A. Pozo Montaño,Sebastián V Taboh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文以运筹学视角，系统综述了社区检测问题，重点围绕模块度方法，提出了一个包含网络特性、社区结构、目标函数、方法范式、评估标准和应用领域的多维分类体系，并建立了统一数学形式化框架，涵盖非重叠、重叠和模糊社区结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社区检测综述缺乏统一的概念框架，分类标准各异，亟需一个系统性的多维分类体系和统一的数学形式化描述，以整合分散的研究成果。

Method: 提出了一个多维分类法（按网络特性、社区结构、目标函数、方法范式、评估标准、应用领域分类）；建立了社区检测问题的统一数学形式化框架（涵盖非重叠、重叠、模糊社区）；综述了代表性模块度函数及其假设与局限；区分了算法方法和数学规划方法两类模块度优化方法。

Result: 形成了一整套包含分类体系、数学建模、模块度分析和基准测试实践的综合性综述，为社区检测及相关网络优化问题提供了结构化参考。

Conclusion: 该综述通过整合分类学、数学模型、模块度分析与基准测试，为研究人员和从业者提供了一个统一的社区检测研究参考框架。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental problem in network science that consists of identifying groups of vertices exhibiting stronger internal connectivity than external connectivity. From an Operations Research perspective, the problem can be interpreted as a family of combinatorial optimization and clustering models defined over network structures. This survey provides a unified framework for the study of community detection, with particular emphasis on modularity-based approaches. We first review existing surveys and analyze the taxonomic criteria used to classify the literature, highlighting the absence of a common conceptual framework. Based on this analysis, we propose a multidimensional taxonomy that organizes community detection methods according to network characteristics, community structure, objective functions, methodological paradigms, evaluation criteria, and application domains. We then introduce a general mathematical formalization of the Community Detection Problem that accommodates disjoint, overlapping, and fuzzy community structures within a unified assignment framework. Building on this formalization, we review representative modularity functions, discussing their underlying assumptions, null models, and known limitations. We also survey modularity-based community detection methods, distinguishing between algorithmic and mathematical programming approaches. Finally, we review commonly used benchmark datasets and discuss their role in evaluation and reproducibility. By integrating taxonomy, mathematical modeling, modularity analysis, and benchmarking practices, this survey provides a structured reference for researchers and practitioners working on community detection and related network optimization problems.

</details>


### [129] [Scheduling of Star Observations under Uncertain Conditions: A Comparison of Models and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13058)
*Thomas Rahab Lacroix,Pierre Lemaire,Nadia Brauner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究望远镜观测恒星调度问题，将夜晚视为并行机器，恒星视为任务，在未知实际可用夜晚数量的情况下，采用鲁棒优化方法评估最坏情况，以最大化观测总收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际星象观测中，气象和大气条件在制定调度计划时未知，夜晚可能完美（可观测）或极差（不可观测），需要在不确定机器数量的情况下生成高质量调度方案。

Method: 将问题建模为并行机调度问题，采用鲁棒优化范式，通过评估最坏情况来应对夜晚可用性的不确定性。

Result: 在未知实际可用夜晚数量的情况下，提出了能够保证最坏情况下性能的调度方案。

Conclusion: 鲁棒优化方法能有效应对星象观测调度中夜晚可用性的不确定性，在保证最坏情况性能的同时最大化观测收益。

Abstract: We consider a scheduling problem for star observations using a telescope. Identical nights are available to observe stars that have been identified as of interest by astronomers. Each star yields a profit if it is observed; in that case, the observation must respect a visibility window and a minimum observation duration. The objective is to maximize the total profit of the observations that are actually performed. By interpreting nights as machines and stars as jobs to be processed, this problem is a classic scheduling setting of parallel machines. In the practical star-observation problem, the ability to observe depends on meteorological and atmospheric conditions, which are not known when the schedule is computed. A possible modeling is to consider that each night can be either perfect and everything is observable, either terrible and nothing is observable. The challenge is therefore to propose a ''high-quality'' schedule without knowing the actual number of nights (machines) that will ultimately be available. We focus here on evaluating the worst case within the robust optimization paradigm.

</details>


### [130] [A symmetric Gauss-Seidel based alternating proximal ALM for generalized Nash Equilibrium problems in Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13198)
*Defeng Sun,Hailing Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带有联合线性约束的单调广义纳什均衡问题，提出了一种基于对称高斯-赛德尔迭代的交替邻近增广拉格朗日方法（sGS-APALM），该方法通过设计新的二次代理函数，在各迭代步中交替求解无约束二次规划来更新玩家策略，避免求解耦合的纳什均衡子问题；在仅需伪梯度映射的单调性和Lipschitz连续性条件下建立了全局收敛性和收敛率估计，无需强单调性或余强制等更强假设，并在风险中性PDE约束的GNEP上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则化方法和ALM类方法在每次迭代中需要求解耦合的纳什均衡子问题，计算代价高昂；而现有分裂类方法的收敛分析往往依赖强单调性或余强制性等强假设。本文旨在设计一种无需求解耦合子问题且收敛性分析仅需较弱假设的高效算法。

Method: 提出sGS-APALM方法，核心是设计新的二次代理函数，使得算法在每次迭代中将各玩家的策略更新解耦为一系列无约束二次规划子问题，并通过对称高斯-赛德尔交替更新方式求解。

Result: 在仅需伪梯度映射的单调性和Lipschitz连续性条件下，建立了算法的全局收敛性并给出了收敛率估计；在风险中性PDE约束的GNEP数值实验中展示了算法的效率和有效性。

Conclusion: sGS-APALM在GNEP求解中避免了耦合子问题的求解，且收敛性分析所需的假设更弱，为大规模单调GNEP提供了一种实用的求解方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a class of monotone generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) with jointly linear constraints. The players' strategy spaces are real Hilbert spaces, while the joint constraint is formulated in a Banach space. To solve such problems, we propose a novel symmetric Gauss-Seidel (sGS) based alternating proximal augmented Lagrangian method (sGS-APALM) which incorporates newly designed quadratic surrogates. In contrast to existing regularization and ALM-type methods, the proposed method avoids solving coupled Nash equilibrium subproblems at each iteration and instead updates the players' strategies alternately by solving a sequence of unconstrained quadratic programs. Moreover, unlike many existing splitting-based methods, our global convergence analysis and convergence rate estimation require only monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the pseudo-gradient mapping, without imposing stronger assumptions such as strong monotonicity or co-coercivity. Finally, we apply the method to a class of risk-neutral PDE-constrained GNEPs with joint state constraints, and preliminary numerical results demonstrate its efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [131] [A smoothing extended sequential quadratic method for difference-of-convex optimization over a convex composite inequality constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13343)
*Jiefeng Xu,Ting Kei Pong,Yongle Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对凸复合不等式约束和紧凸集约束下的差凸目标最小化问题，提出了一种结合变量平滑策略的扩展ESQM算法。每次迭代对平滑罚函数执行一次近端梯度步骤，并设计显式规则更新平滑和惩罚参数。在约束规格下，算法达到(ε,ε)-KKT点的迭代复杂度为O(ε^{-3})。凸情形下，证明了全序列收敛性，并在标准Hölderian增长条件下给出了局部收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ESQM方法无法直接处理差凸目标函数，且缺乏对非光滑约束的有效处理机制，需要发展一种能同时处理差凸目标和非光滑凸复合约束的高效算法。

Method: 扩展ESQM框架，引入变量平滑方案；每次迭代对基于凸复合约束函数光滑逼近构建的平滑罚函数执行一次近端梯度步骤；设计显式规则动态更新平滑参数和惩罚参数。

Result: 在约束规格下，获得(ε,ε)-KKT点的迭代复杂度为O(ε^{-3})；凸情形下全序列收敛，且在Hölderian增长条件下建立局部收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该算法有效解决了差凸优化在凸复合约束下的求解问题，理论保证了复杂度与收敛性，扩展了ESQM方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing a difference-of-convex objective over a convex composite inequality constraint and a compact convex set constraint. To solve this problem, we extend the ESQM in [1] via incorporating a variable smoothing scheme. In essence, in each iteration of our algorithm, we apply one proximal gradient step to a smoothed penalty function, constructed based on a smooth approximation of the convex composite constraint function; and we design explicit rules to update the smoothing and penalty parameters. Under suitable constraint qualifications, we establish an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for obtaining an $(ε,ε)$-KKT point. Moreover, in the convex setting, we show that the whole sequence generated by our algorithm is convergent and derive its local convergence rate under a standard Hölderian growth condition.

</details>


### [132] [Coordinate-wise Polyhedral Method for Eliciting Multivariate Linear Utility and Univariate Nonlinear Utility Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13418)
*Jiaxin Wei,Jia Liu,Huifu Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出坐标多项式方法（CPM）用于切割多面体，具有线性收敛率的理论保证，并扩展到单变量分段线性效用函数及一般非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多面体方法通过求解耦合优化问题来设计成对比较查询，计算复杂度高。本文旨在提出一种更高效的坐标式切割方法，同时保证收敛性能，并扩展到更一般的非线性效用函数场景。

Method: CPM预先指定坐标式切割，然后通过求解线性方程组设计相应的成对比较查询。通过理论分析证明多面体直径以线性速率收敛。对于单变量分段线性效用函数，用增量向量表示并通过线性收敛速率给出Kantorovich距离估计。对于一般非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数，采用分段线性逼近（PLA）并引入自适应断点策略避免方向误差。

Result: 理论结果表明CPM能够以线性收敛速率减小多面体直径；对于单变量分段线性效用函数，Kantorovich距离也以线性速率收敛；对于一般非线性函数，效用函数集合收敛到真实效用函数。数值实验显示，CPM在线性多变量效用函数上与标准多面体方法收敛性相当，在非线性单变量效用函数上稳定收敛。

Conclusion: CPM是一种高效且具有理论保证的多面体切割方法，通过坐标式预切割和线性方程组求解降低了计算复杂度，并成功扩展到非线性效用函数场景，数值结果与理论分析一致。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a coordinate-wise polyhedral method (CPM) for cutting polyhedra with theoretical guarantees of convergence. Unlike the existing polyhedral method, which designs pairwise comparison queries by solving coupled optimization problems and performs cuts subsequently, CPM specifies coordinate-wise cuts in advance and then designs corresponding pairwise comparison queries by solving a linear system of equations. Under this framework, we show that CPM reduces the diameter of the polyhedron at a linear convergence rate. Moreover, we extend CPM to a univariate piecewise-linear utility function by representing it with its increment vector over consecutive breakpoints. We show that the Kantorovich distance between the true utility function and the estimated one obtained by CPM decreases at a linear convergence rate. Further, we extend the CPM to general nondecreasing Lipschitz continuous utility functions by piecewise-linear approximation (PLA). We introduce an adaptive-breakpoint strategy to avoid direction errors caused by PLA. We prove that the set of piecewise-linear utility functions corresponding to the ambiguity set of increment vectors converges to the true utility function as the number of queries increases, and derive an explicit bound for the approximation. Finally, to evaluate the performance of CPM, we conduct a series of numerical experiments. The results demonstrate comparable convergence to the standard polyhedral method in the case of linear multivariate utility functions. For nonlinear univariate utility functions, CPM achieves stable convergence to the true utility function, in line with the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [133] [Towards a Control interpretation of Quantum Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13481)
*Dario Pighin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于控制理论的框架来理解量子优势（QA），通过将量子计算重构为特殊酉群SU(N)上的算子可控性问题，并以最小时间函数的多项式上界来表征QA。在数字量子处理器上的量子傅里叶变换（QFT）和模拟量子处理器上的最大独立集（MIS）问题上展示了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子优势的研究缺乏系统性的理论框架来理解其何时以及如何产生，作者希望通过控制理论为量子优势提供严谨的数学刻画和统一的表征方法。

Method: 将目标量子计算重构为SU(N)上双线性受控薛定谔方程的可控性问题，通过Lie代数论证证明算子可控性，利用门级联引理和标准QFT电路分解推导最小时间上界；对MIS问题，将Rydberg阻塞哈密顿量建模为双线性控制系统，并将QAOA重构成连续时间最优控制问题。

Result: 在QFT问题上证明了算子可控性并得到O(n²)的最小时间上界；在MIS问题上利用可控性结果证明了可在Pasqal量子计算机上求解，并提出了基于控制的量子优势定义。

Conclusion: 控制理论为量子优势提供了系统的研究路径，论文还指出了控制理论与量子计算交叉领域未来的若干开放问题。

Abstract: We develop a control-theoretic framework for understanding Quantum Advantage (QA), providing a systematic route to characterize when and how QA can arise. The bilinear controlled Schrödinger equation is the common thread: the target quantum computation is recast as an operator controllability problem on the special unitary group $SU(N)$, and QA is identified with a polynomial-in-$n$ upper bound on the associated minimal-time function.
  We illustrate the framework on two paradigmatic problems: a) the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT) on superconducting digital quantum processors (such as IBM's ibm_brisbane), for which we prove operator controllability by a Lie-algebraic argument and derive an $O(n^2)$ upper bound on the minimal time via a gate-concatenation lemma combined with the standard QFT circuit decomposition; b) the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem on neutral-atom analog quantum processors (such as Pasqal's hardware), for which we analyze the Rydberg-blockade Hamiltonian as a bilinear control system and reformulate the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) as a continuous-time optimal control problem. By a controllability result, we show how the problem can be solved on Pasqal Quantum Computers and we introduce a control-based definition of Quantum Advantage for MIS.
  We conclude by outlining several open problems that chart directions for future research at the intersection of Control Theory and Quantum Computing.

</details>


### [134] [A Sustainable Integrated Framework for Multi-Type Urban Waste Collection and Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13524)
*Víctor Blanco,J. Fernando Camacho-Vallejo,Yolanda Hinojosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成优化框架，用于城市多类型垃圾收集与回收系统的设计，联合决策中转设施和处理厂的选址及两梯队车辆路径规划，以最小化运营成本并满足投资预算约束，在麦德林市真实场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市垃圾管理面临人口增长、垃圾种类复杂、交通拥堵和可持续基础设施需求等多重挑战，现有方法未能有效整合设施选址与多产品、多梯队路径规划，亟需一个集成决策框架来提升运营效率和环境可持续性。

Method: 构建了一个集成选址-路径模型（location-routing model），同时考虑设施选址决策、多产品路径结构、车辆容量限制和重复卸载操作，目标是在投资预算约束下最小化总运营成本（包括运输、路径规划和处理活动）。网络设计为：分散收集点由小型专用车辆收集至中间中转设施，再经大型车辆转运至处理厂。

Result: 基于哥伦比亚麦德林市真实场景的计算实验表明，提出的集成框架能显著提升运营效率，促进更可持续的回收操作；通过紧凑的收集区域划分、提高车辆利用率，减少了不必要的运输里程。

Conclusion: 该框架为城市规划者提供了一个实用的决策支持工具，有助于设计成本效益高且环境友好的城市垃圾管理系统，同时证明了基础设施与路径规划的联合决策对于优化城市废物管理具有实质性改进作用。

Abstract: Urban waste management faces increasing operational and environmental challenges driven by population growth, heterogeneous waste streams, traffic congestion, and the need for sustainable collection infrastructures. We present an integrated optimization framework for the design of multi-type urban waste collection and recycling systems. In the proposed network, waste generated at distributed collection points is gathered by waste-specific small vehicles and transported to intermediate consolidation facilities, where it is aggregated before being transferred to treatment plants using larger vehicles. The problem consists of jointly determining the location of consolidation facilities and treatment plants, together with the associated two-echelon routing operations, under a limited infrastructure budget. We develop an integrated location-routing model that simultaneously captures facility-location decisions, multi-product routing structures, vehicle-capacity restrictions, and repeated unloading operations. The objective is to minimize total operational costs, including transportation, routing, and handling activities, while satisfying investment-budget constraints. The framework promotes compact collection regions, improves vehicle utilization, and reduces unnecessary transportation effort in dense urban environments. Computational experiments based on realistic urban scenarios derived from the city of Medellín (Colombia) demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. The results show that integrated infrastructure and routing decisions can substantially improve operational efficiency while supporting more sustainable recycling operations. The framework provides a practical decision-support tool for urban planners seeking to design cost-efficient and environmentally responsible waste-management systems.

</details>


### [135] [Differential Geometric Conditions for Koopman Linearizability of Control-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13577)
*Shankar A. Deka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了控制仿射非线性系统可Koopman线性化的微分几何必要条件与充分条件，并给出了可线性化为可控线性系统的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman线性化为非线性系统的控制综合与分析开辟了可能性，但任意非线性控制系统是否存在有限维Koopman表示仍是一个关键问题，需要对所有Koopman可线性化的非线性控制系统进行分类。

Method: 利用微分几何方法，研究控制仿射非线性系统的漂移向量场和控制向量场需满足的条件，在控制不变流形上建立必要条件和充分条件，并引入附加条件得到充要条件。

Result: 给出了控制仿射系统Koopman可线性化的微分几何必要条件；证明了这些条件在控制不变流形上也是（稍弱意义上的）充分条件；加上附加条件后成为可线性化为可控线性系统的充要条件。

Conclusion: 即使自治部分可通过Koopman提升线性化，控制仿射系统仍可能不存在Koopman线性化变换；所提条件易于验证，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Koopman linearization opens many possibilities for control synthesis and analysis of nonlinear systems. Whether or not any given nonlinear control system admits a finite-dimensional Koopman representation remains a crucial question to address. A related problem is to categorize the class of all Koopman linearizable nonlinear control systems. In this work, we present differential geometric conditions on the drift and control vector fields of a control-affine nonlinear system, that must be necessarily satisfied for Koopman linear transformation to exist. The same conditions are also shown to be sufficient for (a slightly weaker notion of) Koopman linearizability on control-invariant manifolds. Further, these conditions, together with an additional condition, become necessary and sufficient for Koopman linearizability to a controllable linear system. Our examples illustrate the ease of checking these conditions, and also shed light on how Koopman linearizing transformation may not exist for a control-affine system even though one can linearize the autonomous part of the system via Koopman lifting.

</details>


### [136] [Optimal Proximity Bound and Product Function Estimates in Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13579)
*Iskander Aliev,Gennadiy Averkov,William Jones,Timm Oertel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了标准型整数线性规划中，LP松弛的最优顶点解到最近整数最优解的欧氏距离上界为√det(AA^T)-1，且该界是渐进紧的；此外还推导了涉及乘积函数∏(x_i+1)的整数最优解界限，并讨论了在背包问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为整数线性规划（ILP）的LP松弛解与整数最优解之间的邻近性（proximity）提供最优的理论上界，填补该领域紧致界限的空白。

Method: 利用矩阵AA^T的行列式几何性质，结合整数格点理论，建立LP顶点解到整数解的距离上界；通过构造反例证明该界的渐进紧性；利用乘积函数∏(x_i+1)推导整数可行解的结构性质。

Result: 得到了欧氏距离上界√det(AA^T)-1，并证明该界是渐进最优的；同时给出了涉及乘积函数的整数解界限及其在背包问题中的具体应用。

Conclusion: 该邻近性上界是紧的，为整数线性规划的理论分析提供了精确工具，并在背包问题等经典场景中有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We obtain an optimal proximity bound for integer linear programs in standard form max{cx: Ax=b, x nonnegative integer}, where A is an integer mxn matrix of rank m<n and b is an integer vector. Specifically, we show that the Euclidean distance from any optimal vertex solution of the LP relaxation to a nearest optimal integer solution is bounded by $\sqrt{\det(AA^t)}-1$ and that this estimate is asymptotically tight. We also derive bounds for the optimal integer solutions involving the product function $\prod_{i=1}^{n}(x_i+1)$ and discuss their applications in the knapsack setting.

</details>


### [137] [Distribution-Agnostic Robust Trajectory Optimization via Chance-Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13605)
*Yashdeep Chaudhary,Roberto Armellin,Harry Holt,Marco Sagliano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机会约束强化学习的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化框架，通过离线计算标称轨迹与在线学习的前馈-反馈修正律，在无需重新设计核心结构的前提下解决了多脉冲地火转移和随机大气火箭着陆两类异构轨迹规划问题，验证了其概率可行性与燃料经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒轨迹优化方法通常假设特定的不确定性分布（如高斯），缺乏对未知或混合分布扰动的泛化能力，且不同任务间需要重新设计核心求解结构。因此需要一种分布无关、可迁移的鲁棒轨迹优化框架。

Method: 将不确定性建模为可采样的任意分布（仅需可采样），离线计算确定性标称轨迹后，通过强化学习学习一个结构化仿射闭环修正律（前馈控制调整+时变反馈增益），利用rollout经验上尾分位数强制执行概率可行性，并通过协方差可行性罚项约束终端散布。

Result: 在三维多脉冲地火转移中，高斯不确定下与现有鲁棒方法竞争力相当；在均匀不确定和未见过的过程扰动下仍能保持概率可行性。在随机大气火箭着陆问题中展示了方法对不同任务（短时域连续推力、含阻力/质量消耗/下滑角约束）的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在保持上尾燃料代价竞争力的同时保证概率可行性，且其鲁棒化核心结构可在异质航天器轨迹规划问题间直接迁移，无需重新设计。

Abstract: This paper presents a distribution-agnostic robust trajectory-optimization framework based on chance-constrained reinforcement learning. The uncertainty is represented here through initial conditions and process noise, with the only requirement being that it can be sampled. A deterministic nominal trajectory is first computed offline, and reinforcement learning is then used only to robustify that baseline through a structured affine closed-loop correction law comprising a feedforward control adjustment and time-varying feedback gains. Probabilistic feasibility is enforced empirically through rollout-based upper-tail quantiles, while terminal dispersion is regulated through covariance-feasibility penalties. The framework is assessed on two materially different trajectory design problems. The flagship case study is a three-dimensional multi-impulse Earth-Mars transfer, where the learned policy is benchmarked against a recent robust trajectory-optimization reference under Gaussian uncertainty and then evaluated under bounded uniform uncertainty and under process disturbances not seen during training. The second case study is a stochastic atmospheric pinpoint rocket landing problem, used to assess portability to a short-horizon continuous-thrust setting with drag, mass depletion, and glide-slope constraints. The results show that the proposed framework can remain competitive in upper-tail fuel cost while preserving probabilistic feasibility, and that the same robustification scaffold can be carried across heterogeneous spacecraft trajectory planning problems without redesign of its core stochastic-control structure.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，用于评估大语言模型作为工具检索器时的真实理解能力。通过生成不同模糊度的检索基准和知识探针测试，发现参数化工具检索存在知识-检索分离现象：模型在标准基准上表现良好，但在更现实的模糊查询和事实知识探测中性能大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准使用详细完整查询并采用约束解码，无法反映模型对工具的真实理解；需要更全面的诊断框架来评估LLM在工具检索中的实际能力。

Method: 提出ToolSense诊断框架，自动生成三类基准：1) 三个模糊度层级的真实检索基准(RRB)；2) MCQ探针基准；3) QA探针基准。应用于ToolBench(~47k工具)，评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB查询上，多种配置性能崩溃下降50-64个百分点，低于嵌入模型基线；部分模型在检索上表现良好但在事实探针上接近随机水平，揭示知识-检索分离现象。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索存在知识-检索分离问题，模型在标准基准下的表现不能反映其对工具的真实理解，需要更全面的诊断评估。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [139] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，在大型有状态动作空间中实现自主优化。在LLM推理优化任务中，Arbor通过编排器与批评器的制衡架构，结合硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议），实现了高达193%的推理吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，而单智能体方法仅提升33%且数小时内崩溃。该方法硬件无关且可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统仅在孤立目标上运行且评估无状态，无法处理大型有状态动作空间中的复杂优化任务（如全栈LLM推理优化），这类任务历史上需要跨多个工程团队（应用、框架、编译器、内核、硬件栈）的协同努力。

Method: Arbor框架包含：(1) 显式搜索树结构作为智能体间的共享工作记忆，随每次测量进化；(2) Orchestrator代理驱动优化，委托Domain Specialists跨推理栈执行；(3) Critic代理通过根因分析、内省和测量验证保障稳定性，形成制衡架构；(4) 将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议）。

Result: (1) 推理吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进高达193%（对比供应商优化基线）；(2) 单智能体无框架仅提升33%吞吐量且数小时内崩溃；(3) 跨多代硬件平台泛化；(4) 运行间方差在2个百分点以内，证明硬件无关和可复现性。

Conclusion: 结构化树搜索作为多智能体系统的认知层，结合制衡架构和技能分解，能够有效解决大型有状态空间中的自主优化问题，相比单智能体方法在性能、稳定性和泛化性上均有显著优势。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [140] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AI智能体的战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用次数，同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误（即智能体在无支持情况下行动但支持本可实质性改进输出的概率），并开发了在线自适应算法和即时校准方法，在多种场景下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持研究关注人类如何利用机器学习模型做决策，但在现代智能体系统中角色发生了反转：AI智能体代表用户行动，人类和工具成为支持机制。这种角色反转带来了可靠性问题，因为智能体错误可能导致严重后果，且智能体行为必须与人类目标和约束保持一致。

Method: 提出一个通过优化问题最小化支持使用次数、同时控制反事实遗漏支持错误的框架。在总体层面上证明最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则，并开发了在线自适应阈值算法，通过随机探索控制遗漏支持错误。进一步引入即时校准方法减少不必要的支持调用。

Result: 在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景下的实验表明，该方法能可靠地控制目标错误率，同时在实际中大幅减少支持使用次数。

Conclusion: 该研究重新审视了决策支持的两个基本原则（支持的成本-价值权衡和不确定性量化的作用），将其应用于以AI智能体为中心的新范式，为智能体系统的可靠性提供了有效支持策略。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [141] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Pythagoras-Prover，一个计算高效的Open Source Lean定理证明器家族，包含4B和32B的自回归模型以及首个基于扩散的4B证明器。通过课程SFT、动态证明推理过滤和增强的Lean形式化（ALF）数据增强技术，Pythagoras-Prover-4B以167倍更少的参数超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B（MiniF2F-Test上86.1% vs 82.4%），32B模型达到93.0%的Open Source SOTA，并在PutnamBench上解决93道题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Lean定理证明器需要大量训练和推理计算资源，原因在于形式化验证的证明数据稀缺以及形式化证明搜索的长推理链，这使得监督微调（SFT）和采样过程成本高昂。本文旨在构建在实用计算预算下高效运行的Lean定理证明器。

Method: 1) 构建按难度分层的Lean验证语料库（简单/中等/困难），用于课程SFT；2) 动态证明推理过滤方案，将每个实例控制在8k token上下文预算内；3) 增强的Lean形式化（ALF），通过扰动已知问题扩展形式化语句变体，无需对每个变体进行正式验证，利用自蒸馏提供额外训练信号；4) 首个基于扩散的4B证明器原型，在推理时迭代精炼Lean证明。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B在MiniF2F-Test上pass@32达到86.1%，超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B（82.4%），参数减少约167倍；Pythagoras-Prover-32B在MiniF2F-Test上达到93.0%的Open Source SOTA，并在PutnamBench上解决93道题。在ALF变异后的污染敏感基准MiniF2F-ALF上，32B模型保持最强，4B模型与之前的SOTA Goedel-Prover-V2-32B持平。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过课程学习、动态过滤和ALF数据增强等计算高效策略，较小的模型可以在资源受限条件下达到甚至超越大模型的表现，为Open Source形式化定理证明领域提供了实用的高效方案。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [142] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive提出一种基于检索的人类驾驶风格化条件框架，通过三阶段流程（风格化人类数据三元组挖掘、轻量检索头训练、VLA骨干微调）实现闭环仿真中非自车智能体的多样化风格行为控制，无需针对每种风格重新训练，在Bench2Drive上相比基线方法提升驾驶分数4.6%/2.5%，且保守到激进风格下速度和加速度分别提升18%和25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶模拟器中非自车交通智能体行为单一（规则驱动或单模态学习模型），虽有工作通过事后标签或LLM推断奖励权重引入风格变化，但这些信号仅作为风格奖励的代理，而非基于人类被明确要求以特定风格驾驶的真实演示数据，缺乏对真实人类风格驾驶行为的直接建模。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(i) 基于图像-文本联合相似度对风格化人类驾驶数据进行离线三元组挖掘；(ii) 训练轻量检索头，融合冻结的视觉特征与小型控制编码器，构建每种风格的数据库；(iii) 微调单一VLA骨干网络，将检索到的上下文点作为上下文行为演示用于航点预测。推理时仅需切换检索头查询的风格数据库即可实现风格条件化，无需逐风格重新训练。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上，PersonaDrive（无条件）相比SimLingo和HiP-AD分别提升4.6%和2.5%的驾驶分数；在风格条件下，每种风格均取得最高驾驶分数（差距约2%内），最弱风格超过最强基线DMW 5.4%；从保守到激进指令，平均速度和加速度分别提升18%和25%。

Conclusion: PersonaDrive通过从明确风格指令驱动的人类数据中检索演示，有效实现了VLA智能体的风格可控行为，且在无需逐风格重新训练的前提下达到所有风格下的最优性能，验证了检索增强式风格条件化在闭环仿真中的有效性。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving simulators typically populate their environments with non-ego traffic agents that behave largely the same way, produced either by rule-based traffic managers or by learned models trained toward a single behavioral mode. Recent work introduces style variation through post-hoc labels on observational data or LLM-inferred reward weights, but these signals act as proxies for what a style should reward rather than demonstrations of humans explicitly asked to drive in that style. We introduce PersonaDrive, a pipeline that conditions a vision-language-action (VLA) driving agent on retrieved demonstrations from a style-instructed human driving dataset, in which participants drive CARLA leaderboard routes under aggressive, neutral, and conservative instructions on a driver-in-the-loop rig. The pipeline has three stages: (i) offline triplet mining over per-style human driving data using a combined image-text similarity score; (ii) training a lightweight retrieval head that fuses frozen visual features with a small control encoder over per-style databases; and (iii) fine-tuning a single VLA backbone to treat retrieved context points as in-context behavioral demonstrations during waypoint prediction. At inference, the same backbone is conditioned on any style by swapping which per-style database the retrieval head queries, so selecting a style requires no per-style retraining while enabling human-style, style-diverse non-ego agents for closed-loop simulation. On Bench2Drive, PersonaDrive (no style) improves the driving score by 4.6% over SimLingo and 2.5% over HiP-AD, and under style conditioning attains the highest driving score in every style within a roughly 2% band (its weakest style surpassing the strongest baseline, DMW, by 5.4%), while average speed and acceleration rise by 18% and 25% from the conservative to the aggressive instruction.

</details>


### [143] ["Did you lie?" Evaluating Lie Detectors across Model Scale and Belief-Verified Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12618)
*Alan Cooney,David Africa,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了13个具有可验证隐藏信念的推理模型生物体以及Varied Deception提示撒谎测试床，评估了四种谎言检测器（CoT判断器、logprob分类器、两种激活探针，包括新方法DYL）。实验发现，在提示撒谎场景下所有检测器随模型能力正向扩展，但在训练模型生物体上，除CoT判断器外其他检测器性能急剧下降，表明当前谎言检测器无法高置信度地判断模型信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练模型生物体无法确保模型确实相信其所说内容的相反面，导致前期谎言检测结果难以解释；亟需构建具有可验证隐藏信念的测试床来可靠评估谎言检测方法。

Method: 1) 构建13个推理模型生物体，通过CoT验证隐藏信念并泛化到未见过任务；2) 提出Varied Deception测试床覆盖多种撒谎动机；3) 评估四种检测器：CoT判断器、logprob分类器、两种激活探针（包括新提出的DYL方法）。

Result: 在提示撒谎场景下，四种检测器在31个模型上均表现正向扩展；但在训练模型生物体上，激活探针和logprob分类器性能急剧下降（DYL保留最多信号），仅CoT判断器仍保持0.82均衡准确率，但部分归因于验证过程偏向CoT可读信念。

Conclusion: 当前谎言检测器无法高置信度地判断模型信念；未来需改进检测方法以克服当前局限。

Abstract: Robust lie detectors for language models could enable powerful techniques for auditing, monitoring, and post-hoc investigation of model behaviour, but evaluating them requires testbeds where models verifiably believe the opposite of what they say. We show that existing trained model organisms often fail this requirement, leaving prior positive and negative detection results difficult to interpret. We address this with 13 reasoning model organisms whose hidden beliefs are verified in chain-of-thought and shown to generalise to held-out tasks, alongside Varied Deception, a prompted-lying testbed covering a broad range of lie-inducing motivations. On these testbeds we evaluate four detectors: a chain-of-thought judge, a logprob classifier, and two activation probes, including Did-You-Lie (DYL), a new method for training follow-up probes. On prompted lying, across 31 open-weight models spanning 2B to 1T parameters, all four detectors show positive scaling with model capability. However, every activation- and logprob-based detector drops sharply on our trained model organisms, with DYL retaining the most signal; only the chain-of-thought judge remains strong, with 0.82 balanced accuracy, partly as an artefact of our verification process favouring CoT-readable beliefs. Current lie detectors therefore cannot support high-confidence claims about model beliefs, and we suggest research directions that may address some of their current limitations. We release our datasets, model organisms, and trained detectors.

</details>


### [144] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TrajGenAgent，一个无需微调、基于分层LLM智能体框架的语义感知轨迹生成方法，通过两阶段编排-执行器设计和异常检测评估框架，在时空保真度、语义一致性和个体行为真实性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹数据采集成本高且受隐私约束，现有基于LLM的生成方法要么仅靠提示工程缺乏细粒度时空基础，要么通过轨迹级微调代价高昂且可能削弱通用推理能力，亟需一种兼顾语义理解与时空精度的无微调轨迹生成方法。

Method: 采用两阶段编排-执行器设计：阶段一通过上下文学习让LLM合成个体和星期条件化的活动链；阶段二通过确定性工作流（个性化POI检索、距离感知位置选择、运动学感知时间传播、LLM时长估计）将每个活动落地为完整访问。同时引入基于异常检测的双检测器评估框架衡量行为与语义合理性。

Result: 在基准数据集和大规模模拟数据集上，TrajGenAgent在时空保真度、语义连贯性和个体特异性行为真实性上优于代表性神经方法和LLM基线，且无需参数更新。

Conclusion: 分层LLM智能体框架可以在不进行模型微调的前提下有效生成高质量、语义感知的合成轨迹，为隐私约束下的大规模轨迹生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [145] [Evoflux: Inference-Time Evolution of Executable Tool Workflows for Compact Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12674)
*Kushal Raj Bhandari,Ling Yue,Ching-Yun Ko,Dhaval Patel,Shaowu Pan,Pin-Yu Chen,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Evoflux是一种推理时进化搜索方法，通过结构化编辑、执行反馈和多样性剪枝来修复工具工作流图，在MCP-Bench任务上将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%，优于SFT、SFT+DPO和ReAct方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在MCP风格的工具调用中经常生成看似合理但无法执行的工作流图，而传统的蒸馏方法（小语料蒸馏）只能教授工作流格式，无法覆盖修复失败计划所需的恢复行为，导致在工具解析、参数验证、依赖追踪和执行环节频繁失败。

Method: Evoflux采用推理时进化搜索策略，将紧凑型工具使用视为可执行工具工作流的修复问题。通过结构化编辑进化类型化工作流图，结合执行反馈、自适应强度、元引导重新设计和多样性剪枝来迭代优化工作流。

Result: 在包含实时MCP服务器和250个工具的MCP-Bench任务上，Evoflux将小型规划器的执行可行性从约3%提升至17-24%。SFT和SFT+DPO方法表现相当、不如或低于零样本性能；ReAct虽然能达到更高峰值，但方差和token成本更高。

Conclusion: 在教师轨迹稀缺的情况下，基于执行反馈的搜索比蒸馏方法更可靠。执行接地搜索（execution-grounded search）是提升小型语言模型工具使用能力的更有效路径。

Abstract: Compact language models (LMs) reduce cost, latency, and deployment risk for tool agents. Yet MCP-style tool use requires more than isolated function calling: an agent must discover tools from live catalogs, satisfy schemas, preserve dependencies across intermediate outputs, and ground final responses in executed evidence. Small planners often generate plausible workflow graphs that fail under tool resolution, parameter validation, dependency tracking, or execution. We argue that this failure mode is poorly handled by small-corpus distillation. A few hundred teacher traces can teach workflow format, but rarely cover the recovery behavior needed to repair failed plans over changing tool catalogs. We introduce Evoflux, an inference-time evolutionary search method that treats compact tool use as the repair of executable tool workflows. It evolves typed workflow graphs through structured edits, execution feedback, adaptive intensity, meta-guided redesign, and diversity pruning. On held-out MCP-Bench tasks spanning live MCP servers and 250 tools, Evoflux raises execution feasibility from roughly 3% to 17-24% across small planners. In contrast, SFT and SFT+DPO on the same search-mined data match, underperform, or collapse below zero-shot performance; ReAct reaches higher peaks, but with higher variance and token cost. These results show that execution-grounded search is more reliable under scarce teacher-trace budgets.

</details>


### [146] [From AGI to ASI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683)
*Tim Genewein,Matija Franklin,Alexander Lerchner,Laurent Orseau,Samuel Albanie,Adam Bales,Cole Wyeth,Stephanie Chan,Iason Gabriel,Joel Z. Leibo,Allan Dafoe,Marcus Hutter,Thore Graepel,Shane Legg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从人类级别人工通用智能（AGI）到人工通用超级智能（ASI）的过渡路径，包括四种潜在路径（扩展AGI、AI范式转变、递归改进、大规模多智能体集体涌现），分析了可能存在的瓶颈与摩擦，并指出AI进展可能持续加速，未来可能不是单一的AGI变革，而是一系列由AI驱动的科学和技术突破带来的社会变革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AGI从遥不可及的 speculation 变成许多大型AI组织的具体目标，其对社会的影响深远且复杂。本文旨在研究后AGI世界中AI的持续发展路径，特别是从人类级AGI到ASI的过渡，为理解和准备这一重大转变提供理论框架。

Method: 文章采用理论分析的方法：首先界定了从AGI到ASI的智能连续体，基于通用AI的理论基础进行形式化分析；然后识别并讨论了四种从AGI到ASI的潜在路径；接着分析了这些路径上可能出现的摩擦和瓶颈，并据此提出具体的开放研究问题。

Result: 本文识别出四条AGI到ASI的路径：扩展AGI、AI范式转变、递归改进、大规模多智能体集体涌现。研究发现，路径上的摩擦影响程度尚无定论，提出了若干需进一步研究的开放问题。同时指出，AI进展可能持续加速，未来社会可能面临一系列而非单一的AI引发的变革。

Conclusion: 面对AI持续加速发展的可能性，传统的单次AGI变革图景可能不准确，更可能是多次AI驱动的科技突破带来的连续社会变革。为应对这一前景，需要全球范围内的大规模跨学科合作。

Abstract: Over the last decade, building human-level artificial general intelligence has moved from far-fetched speculation to being a concrete next-decade target for many of the largest AI organisations. Achieving this goal would have profound and far-reaching impacts on human society, which raises many complex questions for the decade ahead.
